CLAUDE CODE BUILD BRIEF

Bid Factory

A manufacturing CPQ (Configure-Price-Quote) & estimating platform — full functional clone scope derived from fourteen recorded product demos.

Bid Factory is a web application that lets a custom-manufacturing shop turn an inbound RFQ (request for quote) — usually an email with CAD and PDF drawing attachments — into a priced, customer-ready quote, and then into a production order. It automatically reads the customer's files, recognizes manufacturing features and requirements, builds bills of materials, calculates cost and price across multiple quantities, and routes the quote through an internal review workflow.

v2.15 (2026-06-13): all build-blocking open questions are now resolved — see Resolved Decisions & Build Directives, AI Architecture & Feature Roadmap, New v1 Scope, and the Milestone Plan. Where the addendum conflicts with older text (notably the Spatial-first geometry engine wording), the addendum wins.
How to use this document. This spec is scoped to exactly what is observable in the two demos listed below (Precision Cut Industries + Global Shop Solutions ERP, and Arch Medial Solutions). Every screen, field, and behavior below is traced to a specific screenshot, tagged like SS 06. Where logic is inferred rather than directly shown, it is marked (inferred). Build in the phase order given in § Build Phases; treat § Acceptance Criteria as the definition of done.
Reading the enrichment layer. This file is being deepened into the single buildable source for Claude Code. Two provenance tags appear throughout: SS … traces a behaviour to a demo screenshot, and KB: slug links the Paperless Parts knowledge-base article the behaviour is modelled on — Tolera is a DACH clone of PP, so PP's own docs are the build reference (descriptive only; overridden by anything in this spec and always by the DACH delta). Engine internals that previously lived in separate sub-specs (Kalk language, geometry contracts, DB schema, rules JSON, integration API) are being folded inline so this one file is self-contained; a folded block opens with a green folded from … note. Region overrides are flagged in cyan DACH callouts. UI mockups are not reproduced here — see BidFactory-LiveMock.html for the visual prototype.

What the product does, in one pass

An RFQ email is forwarded to a Bid Factory inbox. The system auto-creates a draft Quote, attaches the email and its files, and parses the parts list. SS dashboard / notifications
An estimator opens the quote, reviews auto-created line items (one per part), and inspects each part's 3D model and 2D drawing in built-in viewers.
The system extracts geometry (dimensions, volume, surface area, sheet thickness, bends, machined features) and flags requirements (finishes, certs, tolerances, ITAR) as Review Items.
The estimator assigns a process, material, and operations router to each part; cost and price compute live across multiple quantity breaks.
Markups, margins, expedites, and add-ons are layered on. The quote moves through Sales / Engineering / Material / Outside-Service / Executive review stages.
The finished quote is sent to the customer; an accepted quote is converted into an Order with per-line ship dates, shipping and payment.

The Fourteen Demos (source material)

This specification consolidates discovery from fourteen demo recordings and their annotated screenshots. The demos exercise complementary slices of the product; together they define the build scope. Each screen below links to its screenshot — see the Screenshot Index for the full file map.

Demo A — Precision Cut Industries + Global Shop Solutions ERP

Quote #14801, account DTMA / "Paperless Precision" facility. Emphasis: a very large multi-level server-rack assembly (47 of 1,000 unique parts), the BOM Builder that parses a customer PDF into a hierarchical bill of materials, sheet-metal interrogation & nesting across multiple line items, the costing/pricing rollup with DTMA markup + inside/outside margins, expedite pricing, ERP item codes per stock sheet, and the quote General/Workflows header. Screens: dashboard, quote detail, BOM builder, sheet-metal interrogation, PDF viewer + AI review, multi-qty pricing, nesting config & overview, bulk-edit components, costing summary, quote general fields.

Demo B — Arch Medial Solutions

Quote #15217, account CC's CNC Machining, created from Fwd:_SM_/_CNC_|_Simple_RFQ_2026.eml. Emphasis: the end-to-end RFQ-email-to-quote flow — auto-created quote, Bulk Create Line Items from the email, auto-extracted Review Items across 7 parts, the Part Library historical-match modal, an assembly BOM (Slap Hammer), the 3D CAD viewer with geometric-feature recognition, face annotation + team chat, the Create Rule dialog, CNC operations costing per quantity, layered pricing/margins, add-ons (FAI) & lead times, and the Build Order checkout flow. Screens: dashboard + notifications, quotes list w/ saved views, quote detail + workflow tracker, bulk-create modal, file structure, review-items sidebar, assembly/child BOM, part detail + geometry, part library, CAD viewer + part-setup, face annotation + chat, geometric features, feature highlighting, create-rule dialog, operations costing, costing/pricing, add-ons, add-on dropdown, build order, shipping & payment.

Demo C — Requirements Review (feature-launch webinar)

Quote #8511, tenant "Blood Orange Machine", presenter Hank Portney (Senior PM). A deep-dive on Requirements Review — the GD&T/callout extraction + rules engine that flags critical print requirements so a shop "never misses a critical requirement" and can no-quote bad work fast. Emphasis (all new or much deeper than A/B): the print-viewer "Part Setup" panel (Part fields / "Found in files [43]" tabs, NUM/REV toggle, Whiteout toggles); GD&T & callout extraction (datums, feature control frames, holes, countersinks, counterbores, dimensions, tolerances) with plain-language popovers; Whiteout mode + spotlight; Add Missing Extraction and Mark as inaccurate (the AI-training feedback loop); units detection; the full Create Rule dialog (signal chips, signal-path picker, ADD FILTER / ADD COUNT, AND/OR logical grouping, stacked resolution options, assignee, live preview); the cascading signal selector tree (GD&T / Any text / Geometric analysis / Files / Part / Line item / Quote); quote-level SET ALL bulk resolution grouped by rule; and adding router operations after setting a Machining → Milling → 5-axis process. The presenter builds ~10 rules live against a 7-line-item quote. Screens: part setup, print viewer + extractions, callout popover, whiteout, add-missing-extraction modal, create-rule (empty + filled), resolution dropdown, signal tree, line-item review panel, quote-level summary, process section, operations/router pricing table.

Demo D — Meet the BOM Builder (feature short)

Quote #8987, account "Paperless Precision", user Sarah McAuley. A focused walkthrough of building a multi-level BOM for a fabricated assembly ("002-00001-000 Entire Assembly, Basic Server Rack") from a single multi-page PDF — works identically for machined or fabricated assemblies. The end-to-end flow: upload the assembly print → Split PDF (one 52-page PDF → 52 single-page files) → Lens tags pages that contain BOM tables with a purple icon → assign the parent file → open the BOM Builder where Lens has already extracted the root part, item numbers, part numbers, qtys, descriptions → Add Files auto-matches the 52 pages to the 10 root line items by title-block part number (as supporting files) → Lens finds nested sub-assembly BOM tables and offers "Child BOM suggestion available" prompts that expand a row into a subassembly with children → purchased parts auto-match to the Components Library → Check and Publish → a persistent "Open in BOM Builder" banner remains on the line item. Screens: PDF viewer with highlighted BOM table + title block, quote-files ACTIONS menu (incl. Split PDF / Edit ITAR status), split toasts, Lens purple badges, BOM Builder (initial / Add Files / subassemblies levels 2–3 / complete), published line-item view. SS Demo D 01–13

Demo E — Custom Markups & Margins (Power Lunch #31)

Quote #5722, tenant "Blood Orange Machine", presenter Hank Portney. A pricing-engine deep-dive introducing custom markups / margins — applying markup or margin to any custom aggregation of cost, not just the five standard categories. Walks six live examples on one quote (6 line items, same CabinetAssem): (1) Difficult Material markup (titanium-only subset of material cost), (2) Laser Workcenter markup (one work center within inside processing), (3) Inside Labor vs Inside Overhead split, (4) Piece Price vs Tooling split, (5) Outside Finishes-only markup, (6) a complexity-driven markup where a "PartLevel" operation sets level 2/3 and the markup % follows automatically (30% → 20% on override). Then shows the Excel export with custom-markup columns, the quote ACTIONS menu, and the admin Configure → Pricing tab: the New Pricing Item modal (Markup/Margin, Category, Cost Category Name + Color) and the Kalk editor (get_components() iterating the BOM, set_custom_cost()). Screens: history slides, demo quote, six costing/pricing examples, complexity override, Configure (Processes catalog, Pricing items list, Kalk editor, New Pricing Item modal), ACTIONS menu, Excel export. SS Demo E 01–18

DemoF — Free Part Viewer, Collaboration & Sourcing (Power Lunch #2)

Tenant "Pistol Pete's Parts", presenter Pete Tamasi (+ a redaction/security segment by Chris Darrow). Positions the free standalone Part Viewer as a CAD/CAM replacement for the quoting stage, plus the collaboration & sourcing tools layered on it. Covers: the Parts Library (Team Parts / Shared with Me / Archived; UPLOAD NEW PART) with multi-format / native CAD upload (SolidWorks .SLDPRT, STEP, PDF); the 3D viewer's geometric analysis + manufacturing warnings with configurable, material- and machine-specific thresholds (e.g. deep-hole 8× for aluminum); TEAM vs EXTERNAL messaging with feature-bound @-annotations, Assign Task; secure external sharing (BCC multiple vendors, email subject/body widget, per-recipient permissions — view/download/upload — file selection, and an access-expiry window); PDF redaction that produces a stored redacted copy; the 2D PDF annotate/shapes/measure suite; and built-in supplier sourcing — Tolera Advisor (MSC) free tooling chat, Tolera Source (PEM) fastener availability/pricing across quantity breaks with an RFQ-to-distributors modal, and Online Metals. Screens: Parts Library, Part Viewer (analyzing + results), TEAM/Tolera Advisor, PDF annotate/shapes, Tolera Source + RFQ modal, Files tab + redaction. SS DemoF ep2_01–07

DemoG — Costing Templates (Processes) & Dynamic Routing

Tenant "Paperless Precision" (Sarah McAuley) / "Dini's Demos" (Jack Rindini), Quote #11220. Shows how pre-built costing templates (Processes) make quoting fast and consistent, and how they're authored in Configure. Flow: open a line item → Update Process and Material modal → pick "CNC | Simple Milling" → the system auto-generates the operation routing with setup/run times derived from geometry (Part Complexity, CNC Mill Programming, SAW, MILL-03, DEBURR → Operation Summary). Then Configure → Processes → a process editor: General (internal/displayed name, standard lead time, yield %, unit-price precision 2/3/4 decimals, "Available in Smart RFQ Form", "Use as default purchased component process"), Materials (limit to managed list vs global materials), the Edit Operations modal (searchable op library, per-op Assembly / Root Component Only flags, drag-to-reorder, X-to-remove), default Finishes / Add-ons / Pricing Items (markups) / Discounts, Smart RFQ Settings, and Custom Operation Generation — a versioned formula editor that conditionally generates operations from geometric interrogation (e.g. add Drill Press only if drilled holes / countersinks / counterbores are detected). Screens: process dropdown, generated routing, process General, Edit Operations modal, operations list, Edit Pricing Items modal, operation-generation formula editor. SS DemoG 01–07

DemoH — 2025 "Hidden Gems" Roundup (Power Lunch #37)

Tenant "Blood Orange Machine" (Hank Portney + Meg van Deventer). A roundup of nine 2025 enhancements demoed live, spanning intake → costing → quote output → orders. Estimating-side: bulk Update Thickness on multi-selected DXF sheet-metal files; a Configuration visual upgrade + the operation-definition editor (variables with visibility toggles, display units, ERP code, outside-service flag, operation formula); a high-density operation costing modal (Calculated/Override, primary runtime variables, info-tooltips, collapsible accordions with remembered state); programmatic operation notes (filled by formula, ERP-aligned); a new parts-per-hour (p/hr) runtime unit; and click-to-set dimensions with math-expression + unit-typing input in the print viewer. Output/order-side (Meg): default email templates (per type, with merge fields); expanded Quote Display Settings + a restyled quote PDF; company Unit Preferences & Date Formatting (imperial/metric, inline conversion); a Requotes setting to disable the buyer's "request requote" on expired digital quotes; and the opt-in Facilitate Order drawer with order editing (select-all, Ships-On dates, search, per-line discounts/charges, order history). Also reveals the full Settings tree. Screens: quote files (DXF), Update Thickness modal, operation editor, router costing, CNC Milling modal, Edit Dimensions, Part Setup click-to-set, Email Templates, Quote Display Settings, Unit/Date/Security, expired digital quote, Facilitate Order drawer, Facilitate-Order opt-in. SS DemoH 01–13

DemoI — Bid Factory for Sheet Metal Fabricators (end-to-end overview)

Tenants "Sultans of Solutions" (Courtney Camarillo) & "Paperless Precision" (Sarah McAuley). A 3-minute end-to-end walkthrough for sheet-metal shops, hitting the whole flow: email-forwarded RFQ → saved views → Lens bulk-create → BOM Builder (BETA) → print viewer + Part Setup + Requirements Review (materials, specs, GD&T, CUI flag) → 3D sheet-metal manufacturability warnings (profile "Default Sheet Metal (Laser)") → TEAM/EXTERNAL chat → process→routing/costing (Fabrication; 8kW Laser, Time Saver, Small Press Brake, Inspection, Shipping Prep; "LOW RISK" indicator; "Sheet Optimization Choice" material) → nesting → pricing/markups (Customer Specific + Material/Inside/Outside) → Add-Ons (NRE) → expedite pricing → workflow stepper → branded email send → customer-facing digital quote with lead-time-tier checkout. Mostly reinforces earlier demos; new: the sheet-metal DFM warning set, the CUI flag, and the digital-quote checkout. SS DemoI 01–12

DemoJ — Nesting Parts Within the Quoting Software (deep-dive)

Quotes #7865 / #7850 (Paperless Precision). A focused dive on sheet-metal nesting: assign a Fabrication process + material (type-ahead, hierarchical paths; UPDATE vs UPDATE AND KEEP EXISTING OPS) → auto-flatten + Sheet Metal Interrogation → single-component Nesting Module (auto-populated sheet size / drop threshold / thickness / grain direction / buffers; Common Line Cutting; Outside Service) → generated nest with Yield / Drop / Scrap / Parts-per-Sheet metrics + HAS NEST badge → multi-component eligibility alert → full-screen Nesting Overview grid → Prepare Multiple Sheet Nests (quantity-break-specific stock w/ ERP code + cost/sheet, nest settings, shared-costing lock) → Nest #1 result object (total sheets/cost/used/scrap/drop/contour-length, per-sheet-type breakdown, rendered layout). SS DemoJ 01–10

DemoK — Bid Factory for CNC Machine Shops (end-to-end overview)

Tenants "Sultans of Solutions" (Courtney Camarillo) & "Paperless Precision" / "GOAT Machining Co." The CNC counterpart to Demo I — the same end-to-end flow framed for milled/turned parts: email-to-quote → Lens bulk-create → saved views → print viewer + Requirements Review (materials, specs, GD&T, CUI) → 3D CAD viewer with the milling feature tree + warnings → in-quote chat → process & material selection auto-populating a baseline costing template → cost rollup + markups across quantity breaks → expedite pricing → branded email send (To/CC/BCC) → customer-facing digital quote with selectable qty×lead-time tiers + checkout. Almost entirely confirmatory of earlier demos; net-new: a second milling interrogation profile "Milling (Robodrill)" + a "CNC | Mill Complexity Indicator" process, the Requirements-Review "Double Check" action, the To/CC/BCC recipient structure on send, and the digital-quote "AVAILABLE FROM $X–$Y/ea" range + Certificate-of-Conformance add-on. SS DemoK 01–13

DemoL — Real-Time Visibility Into Purchased Components

Quote #70 (Pistol Pete's Parts), CabinetAssem. Focuses on the purchased-component sourcing path inside an assembly BOM: Convert to Purchased Component via a Smart Match modal (OEM part-number + OEM-geometric + historical-geometric matching, OEM + internal part #, "Unlinked OEM Products", "Create New Component"); persistent geometry memory (a converted fastener auto-tags as purchased everywhere it recurs); real-time pricing & inventory per quantity break from a preferred distributor (DB Roberts/PEM) with green (≥200% on-hand) / amber (at-risk) indicators; dynamic make-quantity refresh (higher volumes surface stock risk); and a Real-time Pricing Settings [BETA] panel (distributor selector, Pricing Breaks: Dynamic + bulk tiers, Inventory Locations). Also confirms the component ⋮ menu (Update process/material/finish, Update workflow status, Copy pricing, Convert, Delete). Mostly extends the Tolera Source/sourcing area. SS DemoL 01–08

DemoM — Assembly Table Updates (Power Lunch #19)

Quote #2128 (Paperless Classroom), presenter Meg van Deventer. Deep-dive + best practices on the assembly component table: single-level drag-and-drop reorder (Grouped off) to match the parts list; breadcrumb navigation + sibling dropdown + unique per-component URLs (deep links by nodeId); thumbnail hover previews; large-BOM performance (>100 components, ~3× faster); and best practices — verify part-type icons before costing, Convert to Purchased + Replace Assembly with Manufactured Component ("part with hardware") to fix auto-classification, Bulk Update Components, Copy Pricing (sibling + cross-quote), Merge/Import historical parts into a BOM, the assembly-router rule (assembly steps only; geometric automation on manufactured children), and reordering quote-level line items. Largely enriches the existing Assembly & BOM area. SS DemoM 01–17

Demo N — Geometric Feature Overview (native engine, no CAD license)

Promotional/feature overview titled "Powerful Geometric Feature Interrogation — WITHOUT EXPENSIVE SOFTWARE LICENSES." This is the single most important confirmation that the platform's own geometry engine — not the Autodesk Fusion connector — does full feature recognition + DFM in the browser, so a customer who has no CAD seat / no Fusion subscription still gets the same interrogation. Demonstrates the engine across six process families, each with its own feature tree, its own manufacturability-warning set, and multiple input file formats: CNC Milling (STEP — Part Features/Hole, Milling Features/Setup+Pocket; warnings: Deep Cut Radiused, Deep Hole, Tipped Hole), Lathe / Turning (STP — External/Internal Cut, Setup, Lathe Stock; warning: Live Tooling Required), Sheet Metal (STEP — Sheet Metal Bend with per-bend Radius/Length/Angle/K-Factor, plus Unfolded Part flatten), Wire EDM (STEP — Setup, Cut, Loop; warnings: Incompatible Faces, Flushing Issue "one away", Angled Cut, Explicit 4-axis Operation, Wire Conflict), Additive (STL mesh — build-plate view with ±X/±Y/±Z reorientation buttons, bounding box, volume/area), and Waterjet & Laser (2D DXF — Flat Features/Piercing listed individually; warning: Tight Corner per-corner angle). Common across all: a feature tree with per-row checkboxes that highlight features on the model, color-coded severity dots + ⓘ tooltips on warnings, live bounding-box dimensions (X/Y/Z colored axes), and volume + surface area readouts. This demo deepens § Geometry & DFM and confirms the engine scope. Screens: title card, milling features/warnings (+ highlighted), lathe features + bounding box, sheet-metal bends + unfolded flatten, wire-EDM features + warnings, additive STL orientation, waterjet/laser DXF flat features + tight-corner warning. SS DemoN 01–13

Demo O — Quoting Assemblies (Connor La Chapelle, Paperless Precision)

Quote #1151, tenant "Paperless Precision", presenter Connor La Chapelle (Sales Engineer). A focused walkthrough of quoting a multi-level assembly uploaded as a STEP file (CabinetAssem.STEP), demonstrating how the system parses the assembly tree, assigns the "Assembly | Parent-Level" process type to the root node, and lets the estimator cost both the parent assembly (its own labour routing) and each child part independently. Flow: upload assembly STEP → the root line item is tagged ASSEMBLY with process Assembly | Parent-Level and its own operations (Assembly | Manufactured, Assembly | Hardware Installation, Sheet Metal | Weld Manual, Generic | Shipping Prep) and operation summary ($58.83 × 1, $294.16 × 5); the Assembly Components panel below lists the full BOM in three grouped tiers — Sub-Assemblies (2), Manufactured Components (4), Purchased Components (1) — with a Flat BOM / Child BOM toggle and "Showing cost without rollup" label for child nodes; Bulk Update Components — multi-select manufactured children → assign Sheet Metal | Laser + CRS material in one action; warning: "This action will delete all existing operations." (note: the longer override-warning text is shown when changing a single part's process via the ⋮ menu; the bulk modal uses this shorter destructive-action warning); navigate into a child manufactured part (Base_Assem.step) → Sheet Metal Interrogation Results (thickness 0.060 in, flat surface area 263.919 in², 12 bends, Eligible for multi-component sheet metal nesting banner); operations router: Part Complexity | Level 3.0 (auto-calculated, billed as a pricing operation), Sheet Metal | Laser, Deburr, Forming, Inspection; back at the root assembly level → pricing summary shows Material Margin, Inside Margin, Outside Services Margin rolled up across all children → Total Price $1,139.35 (qty 1) / $1,672.75 (qty 5), Total Profit $257.84/$383.23, Total Markup 29.25%/29.72%, Profit Margin 22.63%/22.91%; Add-Ons section: NRE (Non-Recurring Engineering) $500.00 and FAI (First Article Inspection) $100.00, each toggled "Not Required" by default; Lead Time 30 Days both quantities. Screens: root assembly overview, parent-level process + dimensions, parent assembly router + Operations Summary, Assembly Components BOM (Flat BOM / Child BOM toggle), Bulk Update Components modal, child part (Base.step) Sheet Metal Interrogation, child part operations router, root assembly pricing summary + margins, root assembly Add-Ons + lead times. SS DemoO 01–15
Naming — resolved. The product is Tolera (domain: tolera.eu). The demos were recorded in Paperless Parts (the reference product). Branding has been replaced throughout this spec: AI assistant → Lens, pricing language → Kalk, partner integrations → Tolera Advisor / Tolera Source. Sample data (account names like DTMA, "Blood Orange Machine", "Paperless Precision", "Pistol Pete's Parts", "Sultans of Solutions", "GOAT Machining Co.", "Paperless Classroom", part numbers, dollar figures) may be reused as seed/demo data.

Personas & Roles

The workflow columns and "review" stages on every quote map directly to internal roles. These roles gate the review workflow and should drive permissions.

RoleDoes what in Bid FactoryOwns workflow stage
SalespersonOwns the account relationship; assigned to quotes; sets expedite options, customer-facing notes, sends quote.Sales Review
EstimatorAssigns process/material/operations to each part; reviews geometry & DFM; resolves review items; builds cost.(primary quote builder)
EngineerValidates manufacturability, GD&T, feature recognition, drawing notes.Engineering Review
Material / PurchasingPrices raw stock, purchased components, supplier RFQs (e.g. DB Roberts), ERP stock codes.Material Pricing
Outside Service buyerPrices outside operations (anodize, powder coat, plating) and gets supplier RFQs.Outside Service Pricing
Executive / ManagerFinal approval on margin, large/strategic quotes.Executive Review
Customer (external)Sends RFQ by email; receives the quote; (future) self-serve checkout. Has an External chat channel.—
Each line item carries an independent status for a set of review/workflow steps, each defaulting to Not Started and advancing to In Progress → Complete (and No Quote). SS part detail, dashboard
Workflow steps are org-configurable, not a fixed list. Demos A/B show Sales Review Engineering Review Material Pricing Outside Service Pricing Executive Review; Demo C (Blood Orange Machine) shows a different set — Status Estimator Material DFM Cycle Times OSV Purchased Components — followed by a NEW STEP affordance to add a custom step. Model workflow steps as an admin-defined, per-org ordered list of WorkflowStepDef; each line item instantiates a status per step. Rules can target a step (e.g. "complete with Outside Processing"). SS Demo C part detail

Authorization & Permissions (decided)

Model: fixed role presets (RBAC) — built-in roles, predefined permissions (not admin-customizable in v1). A user may hold multiple roles; effective permissions are the union. Visibility is org-wide (every internal user sees all quotes/accounts/parts; saved views filter but never restrict) and all internal users see cost & margin. The review workflow is status-tracking only — no enforced approval gate before send.

CapabilityAdminExec / MgrSalesEstimatorEngineerMaterial / Purch.Outside-Svc
View everything (quotes, parts, cost, margin)✓✓✓✓✓✓✓
Create / edit quote & line items (process, material, ops, pricing, add-ons, discounts, no-quote)✓✓✓✓view + annotateview + annotateview + annotate
Update a review-step statusanyany (Exec Review)Sales Reviewany/assignedEng. Review / DFMMaterial PricingOutside-Svc Pricing
Finalize / Send quote · Convert to Order · edit order pre-ship✓—*✓✓———
Edit Configure (processes, ops, pricing templates, rules, tables, interrogations, rates)✓✓—————
Edit Settings (company, quote display, email templates, integrations CRM/ERP, locale, security)✓✓—————
Manage users / roles / SSO✓✓—————
Delete quote / part / component (destructive)✓✓ownerowner———

* Execs/Managers can send/convert if also granted a Sales or Estimator role (multi-role union) — kept off the Exec preset to mirror the "Sales + Estimators + Admin" decision. ✓ = full; "owner" = only items they own/are assigned; "view + annotate" = read + comment/chat/annotate + update their own review step, but not edit the quote's costing.

Export-controlled (ITAR / CUI / EU dual-use): flag + audit, no hard block in v1 — any internal user may open flagged quotes/parts/files, but access is logged (an access log on flagged items). A future "authorized user" attribute can turn this into a hard restriction without changing the model. decision
Build implications: roles are a fixed enum with a static permission map (capability → allowed roles); a user has roles[] and effective permissions = union. Enforce at the API layer (not just UI hiding) per capability. The real security boundary is the org/tenant (all data is org-scoped — see multi-tenancy in open questions). External actors (customers on the digital quote, vendors on an EXTERNAL share) are not users/roles — they use scoped, expiring tokens (see Collaboration & Sourcing). Approval-gate enforcement and threshold-based approvals are intentionally out of v1 but the workflow-step model leaves room to add them later.

Design System & UI Direction confirmed 2026-06-13

The confirmed visual direction is the Claude / Anthropic aesthetic — warm terracotta primary, sand canvas, always-dark sidebar. This section is the authoritative spec for Claude Code; BidFactory-LiveMock.html is the live reference implementation.

Confirmed direction

Chosen from eight live options (Cobalt, Teal, Indigo, Slate, Amber, Orange, Precision Graphite, Claude/Anthropic) against the target brief: "Modern / premium — Elevated SaaS. Could sit next to Stripe or Linear. Professional, clean, aspirational." The Claude/Anthropic aesthetic won on warmth, brand differentiation, and premium feel for the DACH manufacturing context. All component behavior (sidebar collapse, AI governor, expandable rows, inline editing) is also decided — see below.

Design token system

All components consume CSS custom properties. A data-theme attribute on <html> selects the palette; data-mode="light|dark" inverts it. Ship only the Claude theme in v1 — the token architecture supports future re-theming per tenant without code changes.

Active defaults: data-theme="claude" · data-mode="light"

Claude theme — color tokens

TokenLight valueDark valueRole
--p#CC6431#E07845Primary / terracotta — buttons, active borders, brand marks
--p-h#B35428#CE6538Primary hover state
--p-bg#FAEEE5#28140APrimary tint — pill backgrounds, highlight areas
--p-t#8B3918#F0A882Primary text on p-bg
--canvas#F7F2EA#13110EPage background — warm sand (light) / warm near-black (dark)
--surface#FFFDF9#1A1714Card / panel background
--s2#F2EDE4#1F1C18Secondary surface (hover rows, sub-panels)
--s3#EAE4DA#252119Tertiary surface (pressed, selected rows)
--border#E2DAD0#2B2820Standard border
--text#2E2C29#E4E2DDBody text
--t2#6B6862#8C8A85Secondary / muted text
--t3#B3B0A9#555350Disabled / placeholder text
Sidebar color is invariant — always dark. The sidebar background ignores data-theme and data-mode. Light mode: #1C1916 (warm charcoal). Dark mode: #0E0C0A (near-black warm). This is a hard rule — do not use --nav for the sidebar; use the literal values above. All sidebar text/icon colors are rgba(255,255,255,n).

Dark mode specification

Dark mode is a warm dark, not a cool blue-slate dark. Reference: the Claude desktop application. Every neutral biases toward brown-black, not grey-blue.

  • Canvas: #13110E — warm near-black (not #14161C which reads cool)
  • Surface: #1A1714 — warm, slightly lighter than canvas
  • Sidebar: #0E0C0A — deepest warm near-black
  • All borders, shadows, and muted colors follow the same warm-brown axis
  • Semantic colors (success green, warning amber, error red, AI purple) shift to desaturated, warmer hues on dark backgrounds
  • The primary terracotta brightens slightly on dark: #E07845 vs #CC6431 light

Component library

ConcernLibraryNotes
Interactive primitivesshadcn/ui + Radix UIModals (Dialog), dropdowns (DropdownMenu), comboboxes (Command/Combobox), tooltips (Tooltip), popovers (Popover), sliders, date pickers. Unstyled Radix primitives + Tailwind utilities. Override CSS variables to match design tokens.
Data grids / tablesTanStack Table v8All quotes list, line-item grids, BOM tables, component tables, operations costing tables. Handles column visibility, sorting, filtering, saved-view column sets, virtualized rendering for large BOMs.
Server stateTanStack Query v5All API fetches, mutations, optimistic updates, background invalidation. No Redux/Zustand for server state.
StylingTailwind CSS + CSS custom propertiesTailwind for layout/spacing/typography utilities; CSS custom properties for all color tokens (enables runtime theming). Theme switching: swap data-theme/data-mode on <html>.
3D viewerthree.js + occt-import-js (WASM)See Geometry Engine section. Replace with HOOPS Communicator when Spatial is licensed.
PDF viewerPDF.js2D drawing viewer with annotation layer.
FormsReact Hook Form + ZodAll structured forms (modal inputs, settings, configure). Zod schemas shared with the Pydantic backend models via OpenAPI codegen.
Rich text editorTiptapUsed for: Account Notes, Quote internal notes, Quote customer-facing notes, Email Templates body, Manufacturer's Notes. Headless (no default styles) — styled entirely with Tailwind to match the dark design system. Extensions: StarterKit, Placeholder, Link, Table, CharacterCount. Do not use for plain single-line inputs.
Toast / notificationsSonnerStandard for shadcn/ui stacks. One <Toaster /> in the root layout. All transient feedback (save confirmations, file split success, PDF generated, copy to clipboard, undo prompts) uses toast(). Position: bottom-right. Dark theme matches the design system. Duration: 4s default, 8s for undo-able actions.
File uploadreact-dropzoneAll file upload surfaces (Quote Files drop zone, Smart RFQ form, Part Viewer upload). Headless — styled with Tailwind. Validates file type (STEP, DXF, STL, PDF) and size (max 200MB) client-side before upload starts. Multi-file drag-and-drop.
Date pickershadcn/ui DatePicker (react-day-picker)Need-by date, quote expiration, lead times. Already included in shadcn/ui — no extra install. Uses the existing Radix Popover. Format: DD.MM.YYYY for de-DE locale, YYYY-MM-DD for en.
IconsLucide ReactAll icons throughout the app. Consistent with the live mock. Size conventions: 16px for inline/dense UI, 20px for sidebar nav, 24px for primary action buttons. Always import named icons individually — do not import the full set.
Quote PDF generationWeasyPrint (Python, server-side)HTML → PDF via WeasyPrint. PDF template is a Jinja2 HTML file styled with inline CSS (no external assets). Designed + implemented within M5 (no separate pre-M5 session); fully white-label. Output stored in Object Storage; URL on the Quote model.
Responsive / mobile: Desktop-only. The app is designed for estimators at workstations with large monitors. No mobile CSS is required for v1. Do not add responsive breakpoints — they add layout complexity with no pilot benefit. Minimum supported viewport: 1280px wide.
Quote PDF design — folded into the M5 build block (decided 2026-06-21). The PDF template (logo placement, typography, line item table layout, pricing summary, T&C footer, cover page) is designed and implemented as part of M5 — no separate pre-M5 design session. Fixed constraint: fully white-label (org logo + brand colours, no Tolera branding). See DECISIONS.md.

App shell — sidebar

The sidebar is the primary navigation surface. It is always visible; it never disappears on desktop. Key specs:

PropertyValue
Expanded width216px (--sw)
Collapsed width56px icon rail
Collapse transitionwidth 260ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)
Collapse persistencelocalStorage key bf-sidebar-collapsed
Keyboard shortcut[ toggles collapse
Collapsed nav itemsIcon only; tooltip appears to the right on hover (absolute positioned, left: calc(100% + 10px))
Sidebar top rowFlex row: collapse/expand button (flex-shrink:0, left) + search bar (flex:1, right). Button is always the leftmost element so its position never shifts between expanded and collapsed states — making it easy to re-click. Both elements are 32px tall, border-radius: 8px, transparent background with rgba(255,255,255,.07) on hover. In collapsed state the search bar is hidden entirely (display:none); only the expand button shows.
Collapse/expand iconsTwo SVGs inside the button, toggled by CSS — not a transform flip. Expanded state: panel-left icon (.icon-collapse). Collapsed state: hamburger / three-line icon (.icon-expand). CSS: .sidebar.collapsed .icon-collapse { display:none } .sidebar.collapsed .icon-expand { display:block }.
Hover-peek behaviourWhen sidebar is pinned-collapsed, hovering the expand button temporarily expands the sidebar (pinnedCollapsed flag + hoverPeeking flag in JS). Moving the mouse off the sidebar collapses it back. Clicking the button pins it permanently open. This allows quick nav access without a permanent mode switch.
Sidebar SVG icons. All icons are inline SVG — no icon font, no emoji. Key icons: (1) Search (magnifying glass): <circle cx="7" cy="7" r="4.5"/><line x1="10.5" y1="10.5" x2="14" y2="14"/>, 16×16 viewBox, stroke-width 1.6, round caps. (2) Sidebar collapse (panel-left, expanded state): <rect x="1.5" y="1.5" width="13" height="13" rx="2.5"/><line x1="5.5" y1="1.5" x2="5.5" y2="14.5"/>, stroke-width 1.5. (3) Sidebar expand (hamburger, collapsed state): three horizontal lines at y=4.5, y=8, y=11.5, stroke-width 1.6, round caps. Navigation icons follow the same 16×16 / 1.5 stroke convention.

AI Governor pattern (Lens inline content)

All AI-generated content (extractions, suggestions, auto-filled fields) renders at 55% opacity until a human accepts it. This is the "AI Governor" — content is previewed, not imposed. The pattern applies everywhere Lens surfaces a suggestion inline (costing rows, review items, field fills).

StateVisualClass
Pending55% opacity, purple-tinted border, Accept + Dismiss buttons visible, "Why?" link.governor-wrap
Accepted100% opacity, green-tinted border, buttons hidden — transitions smoothly.governor-wrap.approved
Dismisseddisplay:none.governor-wrap.dismissed

"Why?" is progressive disclosure: clicking expands a reasoning panel (.governor-reason) showing the LLM's rationale. This ensures the estimator understands why a suggestion was made without cluttering the default view.

Typography

  • Font: Inter (Google Fonts CDN) — font-family: 'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif
  • Base: 13px / 1.55 line-height — intentionally compact for data-dense CPQ screens
  • Section labels: 10–11px, uppercase, letter-spacing: .08em, 28–35% opacity white (in sidebar)
  • Nav items: 12.5px, weight 500; active state 100% opacity + primary border; inactive 45% opacity (recedes until needed)
  • Data cells: tabular-nums (font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums) on all numeric fields
  • Monospace: 'SF Mono', ui-monospace, monospace for keyboard shortcuts, code, part numbers

Interaction patterns

PatternImplementation
Inline table cell editingcontenteditable="true" cells with .li-editable class; transparent border on idle, primary border + tinted bg on focus. No save button — blur = commit.
Expandable table rowsHidden .li-ops-row TR below each line item; toggleLiRow() JS; expand chevron rotates 90° on .open via CSS transition. Used for operation details, review items, sub-rows.
Command palette⌘K opens a modal search (triggered by clicking the search bar). Full-text search across quotes, parts, contacts, commands. Keyboard-navigable.
Hover tooltipsRadix Tooltip on all icon-only buttons (collapsed sidebar nav, toolbar icons, action buttons). Delay: 400ms. Appear above or to the right depending on position.
Optimistic updatesAll mutations (status changes, inline edits, accept/dismiss) update the UI immediately via TanStack Query optimistic mutation; roll back on error with a toast.

Quote-editor sub-shell

Inside a quote, a left rail lists line items (thumbnail + Part # + Rev + file count) with ADD LINE ITEM ▾ at the bottom; quote-level line items can be reordered by dragging the grip handle beside each (SS DemoM Q&A). A part opens full-screen with its own internal tabs: BOM CAD (n) Files (n) $, plus a part header (thumbnail, Part # + Rev, expand/collapse, assignee, status badge e.g. NOT STARTED, prev/next part nav, "1 of 8 items"). SS part detail, CAD viewer

Data Model

Core entities and their key fields, derived from the screens. Field types are suggestions. FK = foreign key.

folded from DOMAIN-MODEL.md + DB-SCHEMA.sql The entity model (4-layer abstraction + reconciliation) and the canonical PostgreSQL DDL (53 tables, 16 enums, RLS) are folded into this section; those sub-specs are archived. Provenance: KB: assemblies-data-types-and-terminology · KB: analytics-query-builder-deep-dive (PP's field dictionary).

The core abstraction — Part / Node / Component / Quote Item

Four layers, conflated as "the part" in the UI but distinct in data — this separation is the backbone of quoting + assemblies and the reason the deep-copy / replace-referenced-part rules exist.

LayerWhat it isHolds
Parta physical thing (an assembly is also a Part)primary file, geometry, BOM/assembly structure
Nodean occurrence of a Part within an assembly treeparent-node ref + quantity relative to parent
Componenta Part with pricingmaterials, operations, requested quantities, pricing logic referencing the Part's geometry
Quote Itemthe root component tied to a Quote + positionquote linkage + position (it is the root component)

Invariants: root node has no parent, qty = 1, linked to the root Part; root component ≈ Quote Item; child components' costs roll up to it; pricing items, add-ons, expedites, and buyer-facing data live only on the root component. Multi-level BOM = the Nodes; flat BOM = the unique Parts. One Part → many Nodes, all sharing the Part's one Component (a repeated part is quoted once). A Part referenced by multiple Quote Items requires Replace referenced part or Deep copy to edit safely; editing the BOM never mutates the original CAD tree (immutable; re-upload to reset). KB: deep-copy-replace-referenced-part-faq

Spec reconciliation (high-value additions, ~85% already aligned): make explicit (1) the 4-layer split (the LineItem table below conflates Part + Quote Item); (2) ComponentQuantity as a first-class per-break entity; (3) per-quantity Cell entities for PricingItem / Discount / AddOn; (4) the Material 3-level hierarchy (Class→Family→Material); (5) RequestForQuote + RequestForQuoteView (intake + funnel); (6) two Facility types (account ship-to vs the shop's send-from). Roles (Estimator / Salesperson / Component-Estimator) are User refs, not tables.

ComponentQuantity (per quantity break — the richest entity)

Per break: quantity, make_quantity, deliver_quantity, unit_cost, calculated_unit_price, manual_unit_price, unit_price (incl. discounts), total_price, cost split (material / inside / outside / purchased_component / child_override / total), total_discount (+%), total_profit (+ margin %), lead_time, is_most_likely_won_quantity (+%). Resolve calc-vs-manual with COALESCE(manual_*, calc_*).

DACH — money fields EUR (integer minor units + currency), de-DE formatting; tax fields MwSt/USt + VIES / reverse-charge; export-controlled flags reframed to EU dual-use / GDPR; per-quantity *_cell rows normalised (not JSONB) for the analytics layer; part.material ↔ DIN/EN Werkstoffnummer.

Entity relationships (overview)

Account 1──* Contact
Account 1──* Quote
Quote   1──* LineItem            (a.k.a. "part" / "quote item")
Quote   1──* QuoteFile           (RFQ email, vendor quotes, supporting files)
Quote   1──1 WorkflowState       (RFQ Received → Quote Started → Outstanding Work → Quote Sent)
Quote   1──* Nest                (sheet-metal / linear-metal nests)
LineItem 1──1 PartGeometry       (extracted attributes)
LineItem 1──* PartFile           (STEP/PDF/SLDPRT...; one is PRIMARY)
LineItem 1──1 Router             (ordered Operations + Materials)
LineItem 1──* MaterialOperation
LineItem 1──* Operation          (setup time, run time, per-qty pricing)
LineItem 1──* ReviewItem         (auto-detected requirement flags)
LineItem 1──* AddOn              (FAI, NRE, cert, packaging...)
LineItem 1──* QuantityBreak      (qty → unit price, total, lead time, likelihood)
LineItem 1──* BomNode            (assembly tree: manufactured / purchased / subassembly)
LineItem *──1 PartLibraryPart    (historical match link)
Operation *──1 OperationDef ──1 MachineRate
ReviewItem *──1 Rule             (the rule that fired it)
Order   1──* OrderLine ──1 LineItem
Org     1──* Process, Material, Finish, OperationDef, MachineRate,
              CustomTable, Rule, PricingTemplate, AddOnType, Facility, User

Account & Contact

Bid Factory is the system of record (decided); two-way synced with the customer's CRM. Accounts/Contacts are full native records owned/edited in BF (incl. the sales-intel notes), and synced both ways via the CRM adapter — on conflict, BF wins. Each record also carries external_crm_id, crm_source (hubspot|salesforce|sap|…), last_synced_at. Estimators create an account/contact in BF (pushed to the CRM) or pick a synced one. Revenue/quotes-sent stats are BF-computed.

The account panel inside a quote shows this CRM-lite record. SS quote detail

PartGeometry (extracted)

Common

  • dimensions_lwh (6.000 × 3.664 × 1.750 in)
  • volume (11.924 cu in)
  • surface_area (66.216 sq in)
  • weight (1.163 lb)
  • units toggle (IN / MM), editable

Sheet-metal specific

  • sheet_thickness (0.029 in / 0.737 mm)
  • flat_surface_area (29.563 sq in)
  • bounding_dimensions (10.391 × 3.000 in)
  • number_of_bends (3)
  • contour_length (37.051 in)
  • nesting_eligible (bool)

CNC machined-feature recognition (per geometric analysis profile, e.g. "Default Mill"): counts of Counterbore Countersink Hole Machine Direction Pocket Simple Drilled Hole, plus DFM warnings (see § Geometry & DFM).

One per item Lens pulls off a print/model. Powers "Found in files", popovers, and rule signals. SS Demo C part setup / popover

WorkflowStepDef & Rule

WorkflowStepDef (per org)

  • name (e.g. "DFM", "OSV", "Cycle Times")
  • order
  • default_status = Not Started
  • line items instantiate a status per step

Rule (see § Rules)

  • name, description
  • signals[] (tree path + filters + counts)
  • logical grouping (AND / OR)
  • resolution_options[] (one-click actions)
  • assignee (User)
  • scope (org)

BomNode & Components Library

BomNode (assembly tree)

  • line_item_id (root), parent_node_id, order (within level)
  • item_no (decimal path: 2.4); stable node_id (deep-link URL)
  • part_number, revision, description
  • type: Assembly root / Subassembly / Manufactured / Purchased (auto-classified on upload; override via Convert / Replace-with-manufactured)
  • qty: node_qty · flat_qty · main/bom/make
  • cost: self_cost (part alone) · rollup_cost (incl. children)
  • primary_file_id, supporting_file_ids[]
  • component_id (FK if Purchased & matched)
  • child_bom_suggested (bool)

Component / ComponentsLibrary (per org)

  • oem_part_number, internal_part_number, description, brand (e.g. PEM)
  • type: Purchased (hardware/fasteners…)
  • geometry_signature/hash (for OEM smart-match + persistent auto-tag)
  • preferred_distributor (e.g. DB Roberts) + selectable alternates
  • pricing_mode: dynamic (live per-piece) | tier (fixed bulk break)
  • price breaks[] (qty range → piece price), live cost + lead time
  • inventory status per qty-break (green ≥200% / at-risk / unknown), refreshed on make-qty change, with timestamp

CostCategory, PricingItem & Discount

CostCategory

  • name (Material, Inside, Outside, Purchased, Total — or custom)
  • is_custom (bool)
  • color (1 of 8 swatches)
  • cost_formula (Kalk → set_custom_cost) for custom

PricingItem (org template + per-quote instance)

  • name
  • calculation_type: Markup | Margin
  • category_id (FK CostCategory)
  • percentage (flat) OR p3l_formula (dynamic)
  • variables[]
  • order (in the stack)
  • versions[] (editor history)

Discount

  • a PricingItem subtype applied after markups ("Total excl. Discounts")
  • configured under Configure → Discounts (e.g. "Promotional Discount")
Confirmed: line-item workflow steps are configurable via Configure → Workflows (Demo E shows yet another set — Material, DFM, Cycle Times, MEG Review, Team Final Review, + custom "New Step"). Treat as per-org WorkflowStepDef.

Process & OperationDef

Process (costing template)

  • internal_name, displayed_name
  • standard_lead_time_days, yield_pct
  • unit_price_precision (2/3/4 decimals)
  • available_in_smart_rfq (bool)
  • default_purchased_component_process (bool)
  • materials: managed_list[] | global
  • operations[] (ordered OperationDef refs)
  • default finishes[], add_ons[], pricing_items[], discounts[]
  • operation_generation_formula (DSL, versioned)

OperationDef

  • name (Laser, Deburr, Drill Press, Material | Sheet (Nesting)…)
  • setup_time / run_time formulas (geometry × machine rate)
  • machine_rate_id
  • flags: assembly, root_component_only
  • nestable (bool)

Collaboration & Sourcing entities

Channel / Message / Annotation

  • channel: TEAM | EXTERNAL, name
  • message: author, body, @mentions, reply/edit/delete
  • annotation: bound to face/edge/feature (3D) or region (PDF); attachable to a message
  • task: assignee, links to annotation → Dashboard

ExternalShare

  • recipients[] (BCC, from vendor/contact DB)
  • email subject + body
  • file_scope[] (which files visible; incl. redacted)
  • permissions: view / download / upload
  • expires_at (access window)
  • isolation: recipients can't see each other

SupplierIntegration (adapter) & FastenerSourcing

  • capability: availability_pricing (Tolera Source) | advisory_chat (Tolera Advisor) | catalog_link (Online Metals)
  • fastener row: part #, required qty, availability (Available/Insufficient), per-unit / per-BOM / total, by qty-break
  • RFQ: distributor[] (selectable), message, send → email body
  • attaches part file + annotations

PartFile — redaction

  • is_primary (bool)
  • redacted_from (FK source file) + redaction regions
  • versioned; native CAD (.SLDPRT), STEP, PDF, DXF
  • material_thickness (DXF; bulk-editable)

Settings / Output entities

EmailTemplate

  • type: quote_send | order_shipment | order_refund
  • is_default (per type)
  • subject + body (merge fields %%…%%)
  • last_edited_by / at

QuoteDisplaySettings (per org)

  • show flags: quote#, RFQ#, facility phone/site, digital link, part file name/thumbnail/3D/dimensions/process…
  • total_display: range | max | none
  • preparer: salesperson | estimator | both
  • notes_placement: above | below
  • applies to digital quote (live) + PDF (on send)

CompanyPreferences

  • unit_system: imperial | metric (excludes analytics/operations)
  • date_format: MM-DD-YYYY | DD-MM-YYYY | YYYY-MM-DD
  • require_2fa_for_external_share (bool)
  • email notification matrix (per address)
  • requotes_allowed_on_expired (bool)
  • facilitate_order_v2 (opt-in)

OrderAdjustment & OrderHistory

  • OrderLine: discounts[] (percent), additional_charges[] (price, e.g. Tooling), ships_on
  • Order: PO number, shipping address, editable pre-shipment
  • OrderHistory: change log entries (edits, notifications)

Nest

Nest (sheet-metal / linear-metal)

  • quote_id; member components[] (cross-line-item)
  • material, thickness, stock per quantity-break (Length × Width, ERP code, cost/sheet)
  • settings: common_line, part_in_part, nesting_direction, edge_buffer, part_to_part_clearance, kerf_width, grain_direction, drop_threshold
  • results per qty: total_sheets, total_cost, used/scrap/drop area (+%), contour_length, parts_per_sheet, yield%
  • sheet_types[] → layout + components(parts_per_sheet, area_per_sheet)
  • locks operations/make-qty on member components (shared-costing integrity)
  • associated_nests[] (sibling nests per qty-break)

Canonical PostgreSQL schema (DDL)

folded from DB-SCHEMA.sql The authoritative DDL (53 tables, 16 enums, RLS / org-scoping, calc-vs-override columns, DACH fields). Every domain table is org-scoped; money = integer minor units + explicit currency; UUID PKs, snake_case, ISO-8601 UTC. This block is the single source for the schema (the .sql is archived).
Show full DDL — 53 tables, 16 enums, row-level security 
-- =====================================================================
-- Tolera (Bid Factory) — Canonical Database Schema (PostgreSQL)
-- Derived from DOMAIN-MODEL.md. First-cut DDL for Claude Code to refine.
-- Conventions:
--   * every tenant-scoped table carries org_id (FK organization) — RLS by org_id.
--   * ids = uuid (gen_random_uuid()); created_at/updated_at timestamptz; soft-delete via deleted_at.
--   * money = numeric(14,4) + a currency char(3) on the owning aggregate (EUR/CHF — DACH).
--   * lengths/areas/volumes stored METRIC (mm, mm2, mm3) — no imperial path (DACH-DELTA-LAYER §1).
--   * per-quantity-break values live in *_quantity / *_cell tables (NOT JSONB) for analytics.
--   * calculated vs override: store both calc_* and manual_* columns; effective = COALESCE(manual_*, calc_*).
-- See DOMAIN-MODEL.md (entities), PRICING-ENGINE-SPEC.md (roll-up), DACH-DELTA-LAYER.md (tax/material).
-- =====================================================================

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

-- ---------- enums ----------
CREATE TYPE org_country         AS ENUM ('DE','AT','CH');
CREATE TYPE membership_role     AS ENUM ('admin','estimator','salesperson','viewer');
CREATE TYPE account_type        AS ENUM ('customer','vendor');
CREATE TYPE vat_profile         AS ENUM ('domestic','eu_b2b_reverse_charge','non_eu','kleinunternehmer','tax_exempt');
CREATE TYPE obtain_method       AS ENUM ('MANUFACTURED','PURCHASED');
CREATE TYPE quote_status        AS ENUM ('draft','sent','won','lost','expired');
CREATE TYPE qi_workflow_status  AS ENUM ('not_started','in_progress','on_hold','completed','no_quote');
CREATE TYPE order_status        AS ENUM ('confirmed','in_production','shipped','delivered','cancelled');
CREATE TYPE op_category         AS ENUM ('operation','material');
CREATE TYPE calc_type           AS ENUM ('markup','margin');
CREATE TYPE cost_category       AS ENUM ('general','material','inside','outside','purchased_component');
CREATE TYPE process_family      AS ENUM ('SHEET_METAL','MILLING','LATHE','TUBE_LASER','WIRE_EDM','CAST_URETHANE','ADDITIVE','ASSEMBLY','GENERIC');
CREATE TYPE finding_category    AS ENUM ('quote_setup','requirements','features','dimensions','regions');
CREATE TYPE finding_status      AS ENUM ('suggested','accepted','rejected','edited');
CREATE TYPE task_status         AS ENUM ('open','overdue','resolved');
CREATE TYPE export_regime       AS ENUM ('none','eu_dual_use','itar');   -- DACH: default eu_dual_use, not ITAR

-- ---------- identity & org ----------
CREATE TABLE organization (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name text NOT NULL,
  slug text UNIQUE NOT NULL,                  -- e.g. 'fechner' → {slug}@rfq.tolera.eu
  country org_country NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DE',
  currency char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EUR',
  locale text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'de-DE',
  default_tolerance_class text DEFAULT 'ISO 2768-m',   -- DACH-DELTA §4
  export_regime export_regime NOT NULL DEFAULT 'eu_dual_use',
  brand jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,            -- white-label config
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE app_user (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  email citext UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  first_name text, last_name text, job_title text,
  auth_provider_id text,                      -- Clerk
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE user_org_membership (            -- E4-a: one user ↔ many orgs
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_user(id),
  org_id  uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  role membership_role NOT NULL DEFAULT 'estimator',
  is_active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  date_joined timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, org_id)
);

CREATE TABLE send_from_facility (             -- the shop's own facilities (≠ account_facility)
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL, address jsonb
);

-- ---------- CRM ----------
CREATE TABLE account (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  type account_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 'customer',
  vat_id text,                                -- USt-IdNr (VIES-validated)
  vat_id_validated_at timestamptz,
  vat_profile vat_profile NOT NULL DEFAULT 'domestic',
  tax_exempt boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  payment_terms text, credit_line numeric(14,2),
  purchase_orders_enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  erp_code text, datev_debtor_no text,
  salesperson_id uuid REFERENCES app_user(id),
  phone text, phone_ext text, website text, email citext, notes text,
  billing_address jsonb, shipping_address jsonb,   -- shipping auto-filled at checkout
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz
);

CREATE TABLE contact (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  account_id uuid REFERENCES account(id),     -- a contact belongs to exactly one account
  email citext NOT NULL,
  first_name text, last_name text, phone text, phone_ext text, notes text,
  salesperson_id uuid REFERENCES app_user(id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz,
  UNIQUE (org_id, email)                       -- unique email per org
);

CREATE TABLE account_facility (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  account_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES account(id),
  name text NOT NULL, attention text, address jsonb,
  deleted_at timestamptz
);

-- ---------- intake (RFQ) ----------
CREATE TABLE request_for_quote (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  rfq_number text,
  business_name text, first_name text, last_name text, email citext, phone text,
  description text, referrer text, marketing_source text,
  requested_delivery_date date,
  export_controlled boolean DEFAULT false,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  processed_on timestamptz,
  quote_id uuid                                 -- set once converted (FK added after quote)
);

CREATE TABLE request_for_quote_view (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  rfq_id uuid REFERENCES request_for_quote(id),
  has_started_contact bool, has_finished_contact bool,
  has_started_part_details bool, has_finished_part_details bool,
  has_submitted_file bool, submitted_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ---------- materials (3-level hierarchy; DACH DIN/EN) ----------
CREATE TABLE material_class  ( id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id), name text NOT NULL );
CREATE TABLE material_family ( id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id), class_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES material_class(id), name text NOT NULL );
CREATE TABLE material (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  family_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES material_family(id),
  werkstoffnummer text,                        -- DIN EN 10027, e.g. '1.4301'  (DACH-DELTA §4)
  en_name text,                                -- 'X5CrNi18-10'
  aisi_alias text,                             -- '304' (reference)
  display_name text NOT NULL,
  density numeric(10,4),                       -- g/cm3
  cost_per_volume numeric(14,6), cost_per_area numeric(14,6),
  added_lead_time_days int DEFAULT 0
);

-- ---------- process / operation defs ----------
CREATE TABLE process (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL, external_name text,
  family process_family NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GENERIC',
  is_default_purchased_component_process boolean DEFAULT false,
  available_in_smart_rfq boolean DEFAULT false,
  default_lead_time_days int DEFAULT 0,
  deleted_at timestamptz
);

CREATE TABLE operation_def (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  category op_category NOT NULL DEFAULT 'operation',
  is_outside_service boolean DEFAULT false,
  is_finish boolean DEFAULT false,
  calc_mode text,                              -- calculation mode
  cost_formula text,                           -- Kalk (operation-cost context)
  surcharge_pct numeric(6,3) DEFAULT 0,        -- spec: optional op surcharge
  runtime_display_units text DEFAULT 'hr', setup_time_display_units text DEFAULT 'hr',
  erp_code text, deleted_at timestamptz
);

-- process router membership (which op defs a process generates, with flags)
CREATE TABLE process_operation (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  process_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES process(id),
  operation_def_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES operation_def(id),
  position int NOT NULL,
  per_setup boolean DEFAULT false,
  is_assembly boolean DEFAULT false,
  root_component_only boolean DEFAULT false
);

CREATE TABLE custom_table (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  columns jsonb NOT NULL                       -- [{name,type}], alphanumeric names
);
CREATE TABLE custom_table_row (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  table_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES custom_table(id),
  row_number int NOT NULL,
  data jsonb NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE custom_interrogation (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  family process_family NOT NULL,
  inputs jsonb NOT NULL,                       -- DFM-WARNINGS thresholds/toggles
  material_class_id uuid REFERENCES material_class(id),
  material_family_id uuid REFERENCES material_family(id),
  material_id uuid REFERENCES material(id)
);

-- ---------- parts / geometry / nodes (4-layer core) ----------
CREATE TABLE part (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  part_number text, revision text, description text,
  is_assembly boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  obtain_method obtain_method NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MANUFACTURED',
  primary_file_id uuid,                        -- FK part_file (added after)
  custom_attributes jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  geom_hash text,                              -- INTERROGATION-ENGINE §1 signature (historical match)
  export_controlled boolean DEFAULT false,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX ON part (org_id, geom_hash);

CREATE TABLE part_file (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  part_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES part(id),
  storage_key text NOT NULL, filename text, file_type text,    -- step|print|vector|...
  role text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'supporting',     -- 'primary' | 'supporting'
  is_redacted boolean DEFAULT false,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
ALTER TABLE part ADD CONSTRAINT fk_part_primary_file FOREIGN KEY (primary_file_id) REFERENCES part_file(id);

CREATE TABLE part_geometry (                   -- INTERROGATION output cache (metric)
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  part_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES part(id),
  size_x numeric, size_y numeric, size_z numeric,
  max_dim numeric, med_dim numeric, min_dim numeric,
  area numeric, volume numeric, weight numeric,
  raw jsonb,                                   -- full AnalysisResult by family
  overrides jsonb                              -- user dimension overrides (raw vs override vs resolved)
);

CREATE TABLE node (                            -- occurrence of a part in a tree
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  part_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES part(id),
  parent_node_id uuid REFERENCES node(id),     -- null = root node (qty 1)
  qty_relative_to_parent int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  root_part_id uuid                            -- denormalized tree root for fast scoping
);
CREATE INDEX ON node (parent_node_id);

-- ---------- quoting ----------
CREATE TABLE quote (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  number text NOT NULL, revision int DEFAULT 0,
  status quote_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  account_id uuid REFERENCES account(id),
  contact_id uuid REFERENCES contact(id),      -- required to send
  estimator_id uuid REFERENCES app_user(id),
  salesperson_id uuid REFERENCES app_user(id),
  send_from_facility_id uuid REFERENCES send_from_facility(id),
  currency char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EUR',
  rfq_number text, rfq_received_date timestamptz,
  due_date timestamptz, started_at timestamptz, sent_at timestamptz, expired_at timestamptz,
  estimator_assigned_at timestamptz, salesperson_assigned_at timestamptz,
  digital_last_viewed_at timestamptz,
  lead_time_display_units text DEFAULT 'business_days',
  mark_sent_or_finalized text,                 -- 'finalized' | 'mark_sent'
  private_notes text, email_thread_id text,
  config_frozen_at timestamptz,                -- E4-d freeze marker
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE INDEX ON quote (org_id, status);
ALTER TABLE request_for_quote ADD CONSTRAINT fk_rfq_quote FOREIGN KEY (quote_id) REFERENCES quote(id);

-- A Component is a Part with pricing. Root component == quote item.
CREATE TABLE component (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  part_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES part(id),
  process_id uuid REFERENCES process(id),
  material_id uuid REFERENCES material(id),
  obtain_method obtain_method NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MANUFACTURED',
  is_root_component boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  is_assembly boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  purchased_component_id uuid                   -- FK below
);

CREATE TABLE quote_item (                       -- ties root component to a quote
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  quote_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES quote(id),
  root_component_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES component(id),
  position int NOT NULL,
  workflow_status qi_workflow_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'not_started',
  was_won boolean DEFAULT false,
  export_controlled boolean DEFAULT false,
  expired_at timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX ON quote_item (quote_id, position);

CREATE TABLE component_quantity (               -- per quantity break — richest entity
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  component_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES component(id),
  quantity int NOT NULL,                        -- customer requested
  make_quantity int, deliver_quantity int,      -- computed from tree position
  unit_cost numeric(14,4),
  calc_unit_price numeric(14,4), manual_unit_price numeric(14,4),  -- override pattern
  unit_price numeric(14,4),                      -- incl. discounts (buyer-facing)
  total_price numeric(14,4),
  material_cost numeric(14,4), inside_cost numeric(14,4), outside_cost numeric(14,4),
  purchased_component_cost numeric(14,4), child_override_cost numeric(14,4),
  total_discount numeric(14,4), total_discount_pct numeric(7,4),
  total_profit numeric(14,4), profit_margin_pct numeric(7,4),
  lead_time_days int,
  is_most_likely_won boolean DEFAULT false,
  UNIQUE (component_id, quantity)
);

CREATE TABLE purchased_component (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  oem_part_number text, internal_part_number text,
  piece_price numeric(14,4), description text,
  insertion_time numeric(10,2),
  custom_columns jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb       -- org-defined columns
);
ALTER TABLE component ADD CONSTRAINT fk_comp_pc FOREIGN KEY (purchased_component_id) REFERENCES purchased_component(id);

-- operations on a component (instances of operation_def)
CREATE TABLE operation (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  component_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES component(id),
  operation_def_id uuid REFERENCES operation_def(id),   -- null = manual op
  dynamic_name text, position int,
  category op_category, is_outside_service boolean, is_finish boolean,
  is_from_factory boolean DEFAULT true,          -- false = manually added
  variables jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb            -- declared vars + overrides
);

CREATE TABLE quote_cell (                         -- operation cost per quantity break
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  operation_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES operation(id),
  quantity int NOT NULL,
  calc_cost numeric(14,4), manual_cost numeric(14,4),
  runtime numeric(12,4), setup_time numeric(12,4), days int,
  UNIQUE (operation_id, quantity)
);

-- pricing items / discounts / add-ons / custom cost categories (each + per-quantity cell)
CREATE TABLE pricing_item (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  component_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES component(id),  -- root component
  name text, calc_type calc_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 'markup',
  category cost_category NOT NULL DEFAULT 'general',
  is_custom boolean DEFAULT false, formula text,        -- Kalk (pricing context)
  is_from_factory boolean DEFAULT true
);
CREATE TABLE pricing_item_cell (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  pricing_item_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES pricing_item(id),
  quantity int NOT NULL,
  calc_pct numeric(9,4), manual_pct numeric(9,4),
  calc_profit numeric(14,4), manual_profit numeric(14,4),
  UNIQUE (pricing_item_id, quantity)
);

CREATE TABLE discount (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  component_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES component(id),
  name text, formula text, is_from_factory boolean DEFAULT true
);
CREATE TABLE discount_cell (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  discount_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES discount(id),
  quantity int NOT NULL,
  calc_pct numeric(9,4), manual_pct numeric(9,4),
  UNIQUE (discount_id, quantity)
);

CREATE TABLE add_on (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  component_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES component(id),
  name text, is_required boolean DEFAULT false, formula text,
  is_from_factory boolean DEFAULT true
);
CREATE TABLE add_on_cell (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  add_on_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES add_on(id),
  quantity int NOT NULL,
  calc_price numeric(14,4), manual_price numeric(14,4),
  UNIQUE (add_on_id, quantity)
);

CREATE TABLE expedite_option (                    -- dynamic lead times (E4-c)
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  component_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES component(id),
  days_faster int NOT NULL, markup_pct numeric(7,3) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE nest (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  quote_id uuid REFERENCES quote(id),
  label text, kind text,                          -- sheet|linear
  config jsonb, result jsonb
);

-- ---------- orders ----------
CREATE TABLE "order" (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  number text NOT NULL, quote_id uuid REFERENCES quote(id),
  status order_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'confirmed',
  total_price numeric(14,4), total_expedite_fees numeric(14,4),
  currency char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EUR',
  po_number text, payment_method text,            -- po|sepa|card|qr_bill
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), last_status_change_at timestamptz
);
CREATE TABLE order_item (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  order_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES "order"(id),
  quote_item_id uuid REFERENCES quote_item(id),
  quantity int, unit_price numeric(14,4), total_price numeric(14,4),
  shipping_price numeric(14,4), expedite_fee numeric(14,4),
  ships_on date
);
CREATE TABLE order_adjustment (                    -- post-order changes (E? / OrderHistory)
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  order_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES "order"(id),
  kind text, detail jsonb, created_by uuid REFERENCES app_user(id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ---------- workflow / rules / review / tasks / notifications ----------
CREATE TABLE workflow_step_def (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL, position int NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE quote_item_workflow_step (            -- per-item status per custom step
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  quote_item_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES quote_item(id),
  step_def_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES workflow_step_def(id),
  status qi_workflow_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'not_started',
  UNIQUE (quote_item_id, step_def_id)
);

CREATE TABLE rule (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  name text NOT NULL, signals jsonb, filters jsonb, resolution_options jsonb,
  is_active boolean DEFAULT true
);
CREATE TABLE review_item (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  quote_id uuid REFERENCES quote(id), quote_item_id uuid REFERENCES quote_item(id),
  rule_id uuid REFERENCES rule(id),
  status text DEFAULT 'open', detail jsonb, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE task (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  part_id uuid REFERENCES part(id), quote_id uuid REFERENCES quote(id),
  assignee_id uuid REFERENCES app_user(id), created_by uuid REFERENCES app_user(id),
  message text, due_date date, status task_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
  annotation_id uuid, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE TABLE notification (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_user(id),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),   -- source org (cross-org per E4-a)
  kind text, payload jsonb, read_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ---------- collaboration / sourcing ----------
CREATE TABLE channel (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  quote_id uuid REFERENCES quote(id), part_id uuid REFERENCES part(id),
  scope text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'team'                 -- 'team' | 'external'
);
CREATE TABLE message (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  channel_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES channel(id),
  author_id uuid REFERENCES app_user(id), body text,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE TABLE annotation (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  part_id uuid REFERENCES part(id), kind text, geometry_ref jsonb, note text
);
CREATE TABLE external_share (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  quote_id uuid REFERENCES quote(id), token text UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  expires_at timestamptz, revoked_at timestamptz
);

-- ---------- AI / Lens extractions ----------
CREATE TABLE extraction_finding (                    -- AI-LENS-ENGINE §8
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  component_id uuid REFERENCES component(id),
  source_file_id uuid REFERENCES part_file(id),
  page int, category finding_category NOT NULL, type text,
  raw_text text, value text, normalized_value text, units text,
  tolerance jsonb,                                   -- {kind,upper,lower}
  role text,                                         -- basic|critical_to_quality|reference
  gdt jsonb,                                         -- ISO GPS {symbol,datum_refs,material_condition}
  bbox jsonb, confidence numeric(5,4),
  status finding_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'suggested',
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE TABLE extraction_correction (                 -- training feedback loop
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  finding_id uuid REFERENCES extraction_finding(id),
  predicted text, corrected text, corrected_by uuid REFERENCES app_user(id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ---------- e-invoicing archive (GoBD, DACH-DELTA §3) ----------
CREATE TABLE einvoice (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  org_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organization(id),
  order_id uuid REFERENCES "order"(id),
  invoice_number text NOT NULL,                      -- sequential, immutable (§14 UStG)
  format text,                                       -- zugferd|xrechnung|peppol
  xml bytea, pdf bytea, issued_at timestamptz,
  archived_until date                                -- GoBD ~10y retention
);
